## Analyzer Architecture Summary

ai4rag is a Kubernetes component. connecting to external services\: milvus, openai, s3. runtime dependencies\: S3, my-svc. 21 Python package dependencies (21 required), categories\: 1 cloud-provider, 1 data, 1 llm-sdk, 1 serialization. Python k8s client with 17 API call(s).
